Conclusion: 该研究为三维可解李群中的直纹极小曲面提供了系统性的分类与构造结果，分别覆盖了p≠0和p=0两种参数情形，丰富了极小曲面理论的几何案例。

Abstract: We study ruled minimal surfaces in a family of three-dimensional solvable Lie groups depending on a real parameter $p$ and endowed with left-invariant metrics. For $p\neq 0$, we classify all orthogonal ruled surfaces and construct new examples of minimal surfaces. For $p=0$, we parametrize all ruled surfaces and obtain additional examples of minimal surfaces.

### [86] [Linearisation, splitting property and homotopy algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05875)
*Seokbong Seol,Kai Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在分次余代数的框架下研究形式向量场的线性化问题，证明形式向量场可线性化当且仅当其满足某种分裂性质，并给出了线性化同构的显式递归构造。该准则推广到形式流形的态射和$L_\infty[1]$、$A_\infty[1]$代数，分别证明了相应的分裂判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在微分几何和代数拓扑中，形式向量场的线性化问题（即Poincaré-Dulac问题）具有重要地位。作者希望在一个统一的代数框架（分级余代数）下，给出线性化的显式判据和构造性证明，同时将这一框架统一应用于Basto-Gonçalves定理、Bandiera对$L_\infty[1]$代数的刻画，并进一步推广到$A_\infty[1]$代数。

Method: 本文的核心方法是在分次余代数的框架下，将一个形式向量场可线性化的条件转化为其满足的一个显式"分裂性质"（splitting property）。通过给出线性化同构的递归构造（explicit recursive construction），作者证明了这一判据的充分必要性。该判据随后被应用于形式流形的态射以及$L_\infty[1]$和$A_\infty[1]$代数。

Result: （1）形式向量场线性化等价于分裂性质，并给出线性化同构的显式递归构造；（2）由此得到Basto-Gonçalves关于允许共振向量场定理的简洁证明；（3）形式流形态射的线性化等价于相应的分裂性质；（4）给出Bandiera关于线性化（等价于同伦阿贝尔）$L_\infty[1]$代数的刻画的一个初等显式证明；（5）将分裂性质判据推广到$A_\infty[1]$代数。

Conclusion: 本文通过分次余代数提供了一个统一的框架，将形式向量场、形式流形态射以及$L_\infty[1]$和$A_\infty[1]$代数的线性化问题统一归结为分裂性质，并给出了显式构造性证明，深化了对线性化问题的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the formal linearisation problem for vector fields in the framework of graded coalgebras. We prove that a formal vector field is linearisable if and only if it satisfies a splitting property, by providing an explicit recursive construction of the isomorphism that linearises it. This criterion yields a streamlined proof of Basto-Gonçalves' theorem on admissible resonant vector fields. We also establish a corresponding splitting criterion for morphisms of formal manifolds, proving that a morphism is linearisable if and only if it satisfies this property. Furthermore, we obtain an elementary and explicit proof of Bandiera's characterisation of linearisable (equivalently, homotopy abelian) $L_\infty[1]$ algebras. Finally, we extend this framework to $A_\infty[1]$ algebras, showing that their linearisability is similarly characterised by an analogous splitting property.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了$\mathbb{RP}^3$中闭浸入曲面（无需可定向或两侧假设）的定量尖锐估计，建立了$\lambda_2(\Delta+|\sigma|^2+2)$的上界，并通过对临界情形的分析证明了当$\chi(\Sigma)\leq0$时严格不等式成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于推广$\mathbb{RP}^3$中闭浸入曲面的谱估计，在去除可定向性/两侧性假设的更具一般性条件下，得到尖锐的定量不等式，并处理其中的临界情形（即等式可能取得的情况）。

Method: 证明综合运用了以下关键技术：(1) 将$\mathbb{RP}^3$典范嵌入到$\mathbb{S}^8$中（Veronese嵌入）；(2) 共形检验函数方法；(3) Obata型刚性与单调性论证；(4) $\mathbb{RP}^3$中闭平坦极小曲面的分类结果。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了$\mathbb{RP}^3$中闭浸入曲面（非可定向情形）第二特征值尖锐估计的完整刻画，特别通过临界情形的严格性分析确认了估计的尖锐性。

Abstract: Let $\varphi:Σ^2\looparrowright\mathbb{RP}^3$ be a closed immersed surface, with no orientability or, equivalently, two-sidedness assumptions. We establish the sharp quantitative estimate $$ λ_2(Δ+|σ|^2+2)\leq2-\frac{2}{\operatorname{Area}(Σ)}\int_ΣH^2dΣ+\frac{4πχ(Σ)}{\operatorname{Area}(Σ)}. $$ Our main contribution is the analysis of the borderline case, which rules out equality when $χ(Σ)\leq0$. More precisely, $$ λ_2(Δ+|σ|^2+2)<2\quad\text{whenever}\quadχ(Σ)\leq0. $$ The proof combines the canonical Veronese embedding of $\mathbb{RP}^3$ into $\mathbb{S}^8$, the conformal test function method, an Obata-type rigidity argument, and the classification of closed flat minimal surfaces in $\mathbb{RP}^3$.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hermitian几何中长期存在一个猜想：具有常Chern全纯截面曲率c的紧致Hermitian流形，当c≠0时为Kähler流形，当c=0时为Chern平坦流形。该猜想在二维中已得到证明，但在更高维一般情形下仍然开放。本文旨在进一步推进该猜想的验证，特别是在特定的流形类别（balanced三维流形和局部共形Kähler流形）中。

Method: 本文采用Huang和Wan的方法，将其推广到更一般的典则度量联络（canonical metric connections）框架下进行研究。具体地，作者首先验证了紧致balanced三维流形在c≤0时满足猜想；然后受Huang和Wan方法的启发，将猜想推广到典则度量联络，并对紧致连通局部共形Kähler流形建立了该推广版本的猜想。

Result: 1. 对于c≤0的情形，验证了紧致balanced三维流形上的Hermitian几何猜想。2. 将猜想从Chern联络推广到典则度量联络，并在紧致连通局部共形Kähler流形上建立了该推广版本。

Conclusion: 本文在两类特定流形上推进了关于常Chern全纯截面曲率的长期猜想，并通过引入典则度量联络的推广框架，拓展了原猜想的适用范围，为最终解决一般维度下的原始猜想提供了新的思路和方法。

Abstract: A long-standing conjecture in Hermitian geometry says that a compact Hermitian manifold with constant Chern holomorphic sectional curvature $c$ is Kähler for $c\neq 0$ and Chern flat for $c=0$. Although the conjecture has been established in complex dimension two, it remains open in general in higher dimensions. We verify the conjecture for compact balanced threefolds when $c\leq 0$. For compact locally conformally Kähler manifolds, Chen, Chen, and Nie established the case $c\leq 0$, while Huang and Wan recently settled the remaining case. Inspired by the approach of Huang and Wan, we investigate a generalization of the conjecture for canonical metric connections and establish it for connected compact locally conformally Kähler manifolds.

</details>


### [89] [Equivalence of Lin--Lu--Yau curvature and 1/2-Ollivier curvature on weighted graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05939)
*Shiping Liu,Yunyan Yang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明在加权图上，当惰性参数p≥1/2时，Lin-Lu-Yau曲率与p-Ollivier曲率在差一个缩放因子下一致，且1/2这个阈值是最优的。这一结论将先前仅适用于组合图的结果推广到加权图，并由此给出了Bai等人关于Lin-Lu-Yau曲率流全局存在唯一性的简洁证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前Bourne等人仅在组合图（combinatorial graphs）上证明了Lin-Lu-Yau曲率与p-Ollivier曲率之间的关系，该结果能否推广到更一般的加权图是一个自然且重要的理论问题。此外，这一推广可以为Lin-Lu-Yau曲率流的全局存在唯一性提供更简洁的证明途径。

Method: 作者在加权图框架下，通过分析惰性参数p与两种曲率定义之间的内在联系，证明当p≥1/2时Lin-Lu-Yau曲率与p-Ollivier曲率相差一个常数缩放因子。同时构造反例说明当p<1/2时该结论不再成立，从而证明阈值1/2的尖锐性。基于这一等价关系，将其应用于曲率流的分析以获得简洁的存在唯一性证明。

Result: 成功将Bourne等人的结果从组合图推广到加权图：在加权图上建立了Lin-Lu-Yau曲率与p-Ollivier曲率（p≥1/2）之间的精确关系，确定了阈值1/2的最优性，并据此给出了Lin-Lu-Yau曲率流全局存在唯一性的简单证明。

Conclusion: 加权图上两种离散曲率（Lin-Lu-Yau曲率与p-Ollivier曲率）在p≥1/2时本质等价，且该阈值不可改进。这一理论发现不仅完善了离散Ricci曲率之间的关系体系，还为曲率流的理论分析提供了新的简洁工具。

Abstract: In this note, we prove that, on weighted graphs, the Lin--Lu--Yau curvature coincides with the $p$-Ollivier curvature up to scaling whenever the idleness parameter $p\geq 1/2$. Moreover, the threshold $1/2$ is sharp. This extends an earlier result of Bourne et al. (Ollivier--Ricci idleness functions of graphs, SIAM J. Discrete Math., 32 (2018), no. 2, 1408-1424), where combinatorial graphs were considered. This observation yields a simple proof for the global existence and uniqueness of solutions of the Lin--Lu--Yau curvature flow in Bai et al. (Ollivier Ricci-flow on weighted graphs, Amer. J. Math. 146 (2024), 1723-1747).

Method: 作者在最小味破坏（MFV）框架下引入一个轻子夸克场，其相互作用保持MFV对称性。该轻子夸克在早期宇宙中衰变为标准模型粒子，通过CKM矩阵内部的CP破坏相角实现CP不对称，进而产生重子不对称性。与先前尝试不同，此方案不需要模型的任何参数随时间演化。

Conclusion: 作者证明标准模型中的CP破坏在特定扩展框架（引入MFV轻子夸克）下足以产生观测到的宇宙重子不对称性，挑战了长期以来的共识。该工作的意义在于表明标准模型的CP破坏潜力可能并未被完全发掘，为未来重子生成机制的研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: It is often claimed Standard Model CP violation is insufficient for baryogenesis. We present a counterexample using minimal flavor violation (MFV) in which all CP-violating effects arise from the Cabibbo-Kobayashi-Maskawa (CKM) matrix. Our scenario involves a leptoquark field with MFV-preserving interactions whose decays to Standard Model particles yield the observed baryon asymmetry in the early universe. Unlike previous efforts to realize baryogenesis through the CP violation of the CKM matrix, our scenario does not require any time-variation of model parameters.

</details>

*Bradford A. Barker,Jay Epstein,Luke James,Yonatan Kahn,Elizabeth A. Peterson,Anirudh Prabhu,Tanner Trickle,Samuel L. Watkins*

TL;DR: 本文利用第一性原理求和规则推导出暗物质-电子散射率的普适上界，该上界仅依赖于少量材料性质（等离子体频率、目标质量密度和静态介电函数），且这些性质在广泛材料中变化有限，因此该上界几乎与材料无关，为任何探测电子密度耦合的暗物质-电子直接探测实验设定了基础灵敏度极限。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者证明当暗物质与电子密度耦合时，散射率可通过电子能量损失函数表达，而后者的积分受第一性原理求和规则约束，仅依赖少量宏观目标性质。基于这些求和规则，推导了散射率上界，其仅取决于等离子体频率ω_p、目标质量密度ρ_T和有限动量转移下的静态纵向介电函数ε(q,0)。作者还通过解析和数值例子验证了大q极限下ε(q,0)仅随ω_p变化的简单标度律。

Result: 导出了依赖于少数材料性质（ω_p、ρ_T和ε(q,0)）的暗物质-电子散射率上界。由于这些体材料性质在广泛材料中变化范围有限，且大动量转移下介电函数具有由ω_p决定的通用标度行为，所得上界在很大程度上是材料无关的。

Conclusion: 该上界为任何探测电子密度耦合的暗物质-电子直接探测实验设定了根本灵敏度极限，具有普适性和基础性意义。即便不深入了解具体材料的电子结构，也能对实验灵敏度给出理论约束。

Abstract: A wide variety of condensed matter systems are used or proposed as detectors to search for dark matter-electron scattering. In general, the scattering rate depends on detailed knowledge of the electronic properties of these systems. However, when dark matter couples to electron density, the dark matter-electron scattering rate can be related to the electron energy loss function, whose integrals are bounded by first-principles sum rules that rely on only a few macroscopic target properties. In this paper, we use these first-principles sum rules to derive upper bounds on the dark matter-electron scattering rate depending on only a few material properties: the plasma frequency $ω_\text{p}$, the target mass density $ρ_T$, and the static (longitudinal) dielectric function at finite momentum transfer, $\varepsilon(q, 0)$. The bulk material properties $ω_\text{p}$ and $ρ_T$ vary only over a limited range across a wide variety of materials, and to a good approximation, the generic large-$q$ dependence of $\varepsilon(q, 0)$ can be understood from a simple scaling law depending only on $ω_\text{p}$ which we verify with analytic and numerical examples. Thus, our upper bounds are largely material-agnostic, and place a fundamental limit on the sensitivity of any dark matter-electron direct detection experiment probing the coupling to electron density.

</details>


### [93] [Axion-driven spontaneous leptogenesis, precisely](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05279)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者建立了一组包含完全味依赖相互作用率的输运方程来求解不对称性的演化，首次给出了Weinberg算符的味依赖相互作用率。他们还推导了一个简单的代数主公式，能在受控参数区域内复现完整输运方程的B−L产生动力学，该公式适用于任意轴子速度和classically shift-symmetric耦合。研究还考虑了低能中微子质量和两种质量序对最终预测的影响，并通过熵稀释和重子等曲率扰动约束来评估各轴子耦合的宇宙学可行性。

Conclusion: 自发轻子生成的最小场景需要仔细权衡不同轴子耦合的可行性。Majoron耦合因熵稀释问题不具可行性，而与sphaleron耦合的轴子模型在高能标宇宙学框架下可以成功解释重子不对称性。文章提供的代数主公式为未来更广泛的参数空间探索提供了高效工具。

Abstract: We revisit a minimal scenario for spontaneous leptogenesis where the asymmetry generation is driven by a heavy axion-like field evolving via standard misalignment. We focus on the simplest framework in which lepton number violation originates from the dimension-five Weinberg operator and contributions from standard thermal leptogenesis become negligible. The asymmetry is computed by solving a set of transport equations, which incorporate fully flavor-dependent interaction rates for the Weinberg operator that are presented here for the first time. We also derive a simple algebraic master formula for the generated $B\!-\!L$ asymmetry, valid for arbitrary axion velocities and classically shift-symmetric couplings to the Standard Model, that reproduces the $B\!-\!L$-production dynamics of the full set of transport equations in controlled parameter regions. Along the way, we quantify the importance of low-scale neutrino masses and the two mass orderings on the final prediction for the generated asymmetry. Finally, we assess the cosmological viability of different axion couplings by accounting for entropy dilution from late-time axion decays and imposing constraints from baryonic isocurvature perturbations. We demonstrate that explaining the baryon asymmetry via standard Majoron misalignment is under severe tension due to dilution from late-time decays. In contrast, axions coupled to strong or weak sphalerons can successfully produce the observed asymmetry for decay constants near the energy scale of grand unification and axion oscillation temperatures $T_{\text{osc}} \gtrsim 10^{11\cdots12}$ GeV, provided a cosmological history featuring high-scale inflation and efficient reheating.

</details>

Conclusion: 物质增强的暗物质衰变机制为在大体积中微子望远镜中搜索暗物质提供了新的可行途径，双缪子事件作为背景极低的独特信号，有望成为发现新物理的有力探针。

Abstract: Large-volume neutrino telescopes offer a unique opportunity to search for decaying dark matter through events containing a pair of energetic, highly non-collimated muon tracks emerging from a common vertex. Such events would have negligible Standard Model backgrounds and would constitute a striking signature of new physics. Conventional dark matter annihilation or decay, however, is too strongly constrained to produce an observable rate of such events. We therefore consider scenarios in which an excited dark matter state is extremely long-lived in vacuum but decays much more rapidly in the presence of ordinary matter. We present two realizations of this mechanism. In the first, a long-range scalar field sourced by ordinary matter modifies the dark-sector mass spectrum, kinematically opening the decay $χ_2 \rightarrow χ_1 Z'$ near the Earth while leaving it forbidden in vacuum. In the second, the scalar background induces kinetic mixing between a heavy $Z'$ and the photon, greatly enhancing the three-body decay $χ_2\toχ_1μ^+μ^-$ in matter-rich environments. We calculate the resulting distributions of muon energies and opening angles and show that viable regions of parameter space can yield observable event rates in IceCube, KM3NeT, and other large-volume neutrino telescopes while remaining consistent with existing constraints. We also briefly consider the sensitivity of IceCube to multi-muon events produced by the decays of cosmologically long-lived charged particles with masses $\gtrsim 1$ TeV.

</details>


### [95] [GOOFy-compatible 3HDMs and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05304)
*I. de Medeiros Varzielas,A. Kunčinas*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"符号轨道"（sign-orbit）技术，用于系统分类非标准"GOOFy"变换（标量场与其共轭被赋予相关但不等效的变换）下多Higgs标量势中允许的二次双线性结构。研究表明，非零的GOOFy兼容二次扇区存在当且仅当两个独立条件同时满足：底层群具有非平凡的符号特征（sign character），且该特征在标量表示与其共轭的张量积中被实现。该分类揭示了r₀→-r₀流形的重整化群稳定性并非多Higgs势的普适特征，而是SU(2)特殊代数性质的直接结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Higgs族对称性和广义CP变换之外，存在一类更广泛的非标准"GOOFy"变换，其中标量场及其共轭被赋予相关但不等效的变换。这些变换虽非完整拉格朗日量的对称性，但其中一些能稳定标量势。然而，它们对二次扇区的影响尚未被系统分类，这构成了本研究的核心动机。

Method: 作者开发了一种"符号轨道"（sign-orbit）技术来判定与这些变换兼容的双线性结构。该方法在三Higgs双态模型上应用，并设计为可推广到更大的多Higgs扇区。通过分析底层群的非平凡符号特征及其在标量表示与共轭表示张量积中的实现，来确定GOOFy兼容的二次结构的允许条件。

Result: 研究表明，非零的GOOFy兼容二次扇区存在的充要条件是：底层群具有非平凡的符号特征，并且该特征在标量表示与其共轭的张量积中得到实现。关键结论是，r₀→-r₀流形的重整化群稳定性在多Higgs势中并非普适特性，而是SU(2)特殊代数性质的直接后果。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了GOOFy二次结构的完整分类及其不变双线性子空间，揭示了重整化群稳定性的代数障碍。这些结果对理解多Higgs模型中非标准对称变换的结构性限制具有重要理论意义。

Abstract: Beyond conventional Higgs-family and general CP transformations, one may also consider a broader class of non-standard "GOOFy" transformations, in which the scalar fields and their conjugates are assigned related but inequivalent transformations. Although these transformations are not symmetries of a full Lagrangian in the conventional sense, some nevertheless stabilise the scalar potential. Their impact on the quadratic sector has not yet been systematically classified. We develop a sign-orbit technique to determine the bilinear structures compatible with these transformations. Applied to three-Higgs-doublet models, and formulated so as to extend to larger multi-Higgs sectors, the method shows that a non-vanishing GOOFy-compatible quadratic sector exists only when two independent conditions are met: the underlying group admits a non-trivial sign character, and that character is realised within the tensor product of the scalar representation and its conjugate. As a key consequence, we demonstrate that the renormalisation-group stability of the $r_0 \to -r_0$ manifold in isolation is not a generic feature of multi-Higgs potentials, but rather a direct consequence of special algebraic properties of $SU(2)$. These results provide a classification of the admissible GOOFy quadratic structures and their invariant bilinear subspaces, highlighting the algebraic obstructions to their renormalisation-group stability.

</details>


### [96] [Beyond Borel Windows: A Systematic Optimization Framework for QCD Sum Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05322)
*Raphael M. Albuquerque*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统化的QCD Laplace求和规则优化框架用于确定求和窗口，通过结合OPE熵判据、局域质量平稳性和辅助参数（τ、tc、θ）的关联分析，替代传统的固定收敛百分比和视觉平台选择方法。应用于最轻全粲四夸克态J^PC=2^{++}，给出质量预测M=(6.32±0.11) GeV，与ATLAS和CMS观测到的近阈值共振结构相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的QCD求和规则中，求和窗口的选取主要依赖固定的收敛百分比和经验性的"平台"视觉选择，缺乏系统性和可复现性，导致结果对主观判断敏感。作者旨在建立一个可复现、定量化的框架来确定求和规则窗口，从而减少辅助参数选择带来的系统误差。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心步骤：(1)引入归一化OPE熵，量化微扰与非微扰QCD贡献之间的再分配，以此界定初始Working Region；(2)通过最小化质量估计量的局域残差变化来精炼该区域；(3)要求质量对连续阈值tc和插值流混合角θ具有弱敏感性。将这三个判据协同应用于全粲四夸克态2^{++}的分析，使用混合diquark-antidiquark与meson-meson插值流。

Result: 优化选出θ=17.2°±0.4°，√tc=7.08±0.08 GeV，τ=0.410±0.010 GeV^{-2}，预测最轻全粲四夸克态J^PC=2^{++}质量为(6.32±0.11) GeV。该态位于di-J/ψ谱的近阈值区域，在误差范围内与ATLAS报道的最低共振组分以及CMS近期以BW0参数化的增强结构兼容。该方法能可复现地识别辅助参数敏感性降低的有限域，并将残余依赖性纳入最终不确定性。

Conclusion: 该框架为QCD求和规则中求和窗口的确定提供了一种可复现、系统化的替代方案，能够有效降低辅助参数选择的主观性，并合理量化剩余参数依赖带来的不确定性。对全粲四夸克态的应用结果与当前实验观测一致，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a systematic optimization framework for determining the sum rule window in QCD Laplace sum rules. Instead of relying primarily on fixed convergence percentages and visual plateau selection, the procedure combines an OPE entropy criterion, local mass stationarity, and a correlated analysis of the Laplace parameter $τ$, the continuum threshold $t_c$, and the interpolating-current mixing angle $θ$. The normalized OPE entropy is introduced to quantify the redistribution of the QCD contributions between the perturbative and nonperturbative sectors and to delimit an initial Working Region. This domain is subsequently refined by minimizing the residual variation of the mass estimator and requiring weak sensitivity to the continuum threshold and to the current composition. As an application, we study the lowest fully charmed tetraquark state with quantum numbers $J^{PC}=2^{++}$ using a mixed diquark--antidiquark and meson--meson interpolating current. The optimization selects $θ=17.2^\circ\pm0.4^\circ$, $\sqrt{t_c}=7.08\pm0.08~\mathrm{GeV}$, and $τ=0.410\pm0.010~\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}$, leading to the mass prediction $M_{c\bar c c\bar c}^{2^{++}}=(6.32\pm0.11)~\mathrm{GeV}$. The predicted state lies in the near-threshold region of the di-$J/ψ$ spectrum and is compatible, within uncertainties, with the lowest resonant component reported by ATLAS and with the enhancement parametrized as $BW_0$ in the recent CMS publication. The proposed framework provides a reproducible way of identifying finite domains of reduced auxiliary-parameter sensitivity and of incorporating the remaining dependence into the final uncertainty.

</details>


### [97] [Photon-nucleon entanglement in Compton scattering at low and high energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05330)
*Yoshitaka Hatta,Víctor Martínez-Fernández*

TL;DR: 本文研究了Compton散射中终态光子-核子系统（γ-N）的自旋-自旋纠缠，涵盖低能（低于π介子阈值）和高能（微扰QCD到次领头阶）两个能量区域。作者首先建立了一个不-go定理：对于任意自旋-1/2靶粒子，若散射振幅为实数，则非极化Compton散射无法产生纠缠。随后考虑极化Compton散射，在低能区域发现了丰富的最大纠缠Bell态及其幺正等价态，并揭示了质子和中子靶纠缠模式的不同，其中中子的电极化率和磁极化率对纠缠模式有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子纠缠作为研究核子（质子和中子）电磁结构的新工具。传统上，核子的电磁性质通过Compton散射的微分截面等可观测量来研究；本文提出利用自旋纠缠这一量子信息视角来揭示核子的电磁极化性质，为核子结构研究提供全新的观测途径。

Method: 通过两个能量区域分析Compton散射γ-N体系的纠缠特性：(1) 低能区域（Chiral Effective Field Theory描述）和(2) 高能区域（微扰QCD到NLO）。首先证明不-go定理（实振幅下非极化散射无纠缠），然后分析极化散射下纠缠的动力学行为，并对比电子、质子和中子靶的纠缠模式。

Result: 低能区域发现了多个最大纠缠Bell态及其幺正等价态，分布在运动学平面的不同区域。质子和中子展现出不同的纠缠模式——中子的纠缠存在与否及其模式强烈依赖于其电极化率和磁极化率，这表明纠缠可作为探测核子电磁性质的灵敏探针。

Conclusion: 自旋-自旋纠缠为研究核子电磁性质提供了一种新颖且灵敏的工具。特别是中子靶的纠缠行为对极化率极其敏感，这可能启发未来利用纠缠测量来更精确地约束核子结构参数，补充传统Compton散射测量手段。

Abstract: We study spin-spin entanglement in the final state photon-nucleon system in Compton scattering, both at low energy below the pion threshold and at high energy in perturbative QCD to next-to-leading order. We first establish a no-go theorem showing that, for any spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ target, entanglement cannot be generated in unpolarized Compton scattering if the scattering amplitudes are real. We then consider polarized Compton scattering off the electron, the proton and the neutron. At low energy, we uncover a rich variety of maximally entangled Bell states and their unitary equivalents realized across different regions of the kinematic plane. Interestingly, the proton and neutron targets exhibit distinct patterns of entanglement. In the neutron case, the electric and magnetic polarizabilities dramatically influence the pattern and even the existence of entanglement. This suggests that entanglement can serve as a novel tool for investigating the detailed electromagnetic properties of the nucleons.

</details>

Conclusion: HiggsTools经过本次更新，已为LHC Run 3和HL-LHC时代的BSM标量现象学研究做好了充分准备，能够在更高能量、更多搜索通道和更高精度的实验数据下，为物理学家提供更强大和完善的预测与约束工具。

Abstract: HiggsTools, including the subpackages HiggsPredictions, HiggsBounds, and HiggsSignals, is a toolbox for Beyond-the-SM (BSM) scalar phenomenology at the LHC. It provides BSM model predictions, tests the model against experimental limits from searches for BSM scalars and derives constraints from the measurements of the properties of the discovered Higgs boson. We present a variety of improvements to the HiggsTools framework, preparing it for the results of LHC Run 3 and the HL-LHC. HiggsPredictions now provides additional cross-section predictions for centre-of-mass energies of 13.6 and 14 TeV. Moreover, it now includes cross-section predictions for resonant and non-resonant Higgs-boson pair production. For HiggsBounds, we describe the recasting of searches using multi-top final states, explain their implementation and highlight the impact of the experimental sensitivity of those results. Furthermore, we discuss the implementation of coupling-dependent limits on non-resonant Higgs boson pair production, as well as the improved handling of searches conducted prior to the Higgs boson discovery. For HiggsSignals we describe several improvements for the case of scalars with mass uncertainties.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隧穿研究多基于相对论量子力学（RQM）框架，缺乏考虑场论效应的完整描述。作者希望建立一种非微扰QFT计算方法，既能处理任意势垒的数值模拟，又能对特定势垒给出解析精确解，从而填补RQM与完整QFT之间的空白。

Method: 采用位置空间势（position space potential）形式论，将旋量隧穿问题转化为耦合递推关系，这些递推关系构成自洽的积分方程系统。该方法同时支持解析与数值求解：对任意势垒可进行数值计算，对特定势垒（如矩形势垒）可得到解析精确结果。

Result: 对于矩形势垒，QFT导出的隧穿振幅与相对论量子力学的计算结果完全一致，验证了该形式论的正确性，并确认其作为计算超越RQM的场论修正的基础是可靠的。

Conclusion: 该非微扰QFT形式论为旋量隧穿问题提供了统一的求解框架，矩形势垒验证结果表明其与现有RQM理论自洽兼容，未来可用于计算包含场论修正（如多粒子产生、辐射修正等）的隧穿过程。

Abstract: This work presents a non-perturbative quantum field theoretic computation of spinor tunnelling through a potential barrier. Within position space potential formalism, we identify coupled recursion relations forming a self-consistent integral equation. Our method, enabling both analytical and numerical approaches, transforms into a formalism admitting both numerical framework for arbitrary potentials and analytical exact results for select barriers. For the rectangular barrier, the QFT-derived amplitudes reproduce the relativistic quantum mechanical (RQM) results, thus establishing a foundation for future calculation of beyond-RQM field-theoretic corrections.

</details>

Method: 在膨胀等离子体中推导轴子场均值衰减与随机动量模产生的耦合方程；引入单一动量依赖光学深度将各模划分为相干功率与Bose占据两部分；考虑有限空间和时间响应以抑制高动量产生；并研究弱耦合实现下的暗物质丰度、相干等曲率扰动以及产生关联尺度。

Result: 随机遗留轴子具有比相同质量和浴温下平衡分布更低的平均动量与更短的自由流长；弱耦合实现可产生暗物质丰度、降低继承的相干等曲率扰动，并将产生关联限制在无碰撞传播可抹平的尺度内。

Conclusion: 热弛豫决定了轴子暗物质的组成、动量分布及输运的结构，将标准错位、部分弛豫和随机产生统一于同一动力学关系之中。

Abstract: An axion driven toward a thermal minimum need not remain a coherent condensate. We derive the coupled attenuation of the field mean and production of stochastic momentum modes in an expanding plasma. A single momentum dependent optical depth partitions every mode between coherent power and Bose occupation, placing standard misalignment, partial relaxation, and stochastic production in one dynamical relation. Finite spatial and temporal response suppresses high momentum production, so the stochastic relic has lower mean momentum and a shorter free streaming length than an equilibrium population at fixed mass and bath temperature. A weakly coupled realization produces the dark matter abundance, reduces inherited coherent isocurvature, and confines production correlations to scales removed by collisionless propagation. Thermal relaxation therefore determines the composition, momentum distribution, and transported structure of axion dark matter.

</details>


### [101] [Revisiting the $Λ_c^+ \to nπ^+η$ decay in light of the BESIII measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05629)
*Meng-Yuan Li,Jing Tang,Wen-Tao Lyu,Shi-Chen Xue,En Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于最新的BESIII实验测量数据，对Λ_c^+→nπ^+η衰变过程进行了系统的理论研究。研究考虑了由S波赝标介子-八重态重子相互作用动态产生的N(1535)态、源于S波赝标介子-赝标介子相互作用的a_0(980)共振，以及中间态N(1440)和a_2(1320)的贡献。结果表明a_0(980)对该过程有显著贡献，核子共振态在描述π^+η不变质量谱（低能和高能区域）中起关键作用，但由于实验统计量不足和33 MeV的粗bin尺寸，无法可靠提取a_0(980)的精确贡献分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最新的BESIII实验对Λ_c^+→nπ^+η衰变提供了新测量数据，这为研究低能区强子物理中重要的动力学机制（如动态产生的N(1535)态和a_0(980)共振）提供了实验契机。作者希望通过系统的理论分析，深入理解这些共振态在该衰变过程中的角色，并揭示a_0(980)的内在性质。

Method: 采用唯象模型对Λ_c^+→nπ^+η衰变进行系统研究，纳入以下贡献：(1)由S波赝标介子-八重态重子相互作用动态产生的N(1535)态；(2)源于S波赝标介子-赝标介子相互作用的a_0(980)共振；(3)中间态N(1440)；(4)中间态a_2(1320)。通过对实验数据（π^+η不变质量谱）进行拟合来确定各贡献的相对强度。

Result: a_0(980)对该衰变过程提供显著贡献；加入a_2(1320)对拟合质量几乎没有改善；核子共振态（N(1535)和N(1440)）在描述π^+η不变质量谱的低能和高能区域行为中均起关键作用。然而，受限于实验统计量不足和33 MeV的粗bin尺寸，无法可靠地提取a_0(980)的精确贡献分数。

Conclusion: 本文建议未来开展更高精度和更高统计量的Λ_c^+→nπ^+η实验测量，这有助于揭示a_0(980)的内在本质（如分子态或四夸克态等），并定量评估不同激发核子态在该衰变中的具体作用。

Abstract: Motivated by the latest BESIII measurements on $Λ_c^+\to nπ^+η$, we perform a systematic theoretical study of this decay. We take into account contributions from the $N(1535)$ state dynamically generated by $S$-wave pseudoscalar meson-octet baryon interactions, the $a_0(980)$ resonance originating from the $S$-wave pseudoscalar meson-pseudoscalar meson interactions, together with the intermediate states $N(1440)$ and $a_2(1320)$. Our results indicate that $a_0(980)$ provides a significant contribution to this process. The inclusion of $a_2(1320)$ hardly improves the fitting quality, while the nucleon resonances play a crucial role in describing the experimental behavior of the $π^+η$ invariant mass spectrum in both low and high energy regions. Restricted by insufficient experimental statistics and a coarse bin size of 33 MeV, the precise contribution fraction of $a_0(980)$ cannot be reliably extracted. We propose future higher-precision and higher-statistics experimental measurements of $Λ_c^+\to nπ^+η$, which can help reveal the intrinsic nature of $a_0(980)$ and quantify the roles of different excited nucleon states in this decay.

</details>


### [102] [Net electron spin rotation in a plane-wave pulse: Holonomy set by the anomalous magnetic moment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05698)
*N. S. Akintsov,A. P. Nevecheria,S. N. Andreev,Qing-Hua Qin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文从理论上计算了相对论电子穿过有限持续时间平面波激光脉冲后存活的电子自旋旋转角度。研究发现，在精确g=2演化的相互作用图像中，Thomas-Bargmann-Michel-Telegdi方程退化为偏振平面上具有常数系数的联络的平行输运，净旋转角度为-½a_e²A，其中a_e为异常磁矩(g-2)/2，A为横向矢量势闭合曲线所围面积的两倍。该旋转角度通过异常磁矩耦合来度量光的螺旋度，并验证了与中性磁偶极子取向记忆的类比关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索相对论电子穿越有限持续时间激光脉冲后自旋旋转的精确物理机制，揭示旋转与光脉冲所携带的自旋角动量（光螺旋度）之间的定量关系，并理解异常磁矩在该过程中的作用。

Method: 采用精确g=2演化的相互作用图像，将Thomas-BMB方程转化为偏振平面上具有常数系数的联络的平行输运问题；通过几何相位（holonomy）方法计算净旋转角；借助Volkov轨道分析带电电子的耦合抵消效应；通过数值模拟验证g=2时的抵消（180个脉冲构型）和面积律（89个构型）。

Result: 净旋转角为-½a_e²A，其中a_e=(g-2)/2为异常磁矩，A为横向矢量势闭合曲线有向面积的两倍（即脉冲每单位面积携带的自旋角动量）。旋转角唯一地由异常磁矩或g/2耦合决定；对于带电电子在Volkov轨道上，g/2耦合完全抵消，抑制系数为1.3×10⁻⁶；有限聚焦在1/kw₀的二阶处恢复无g无关部分，且在γ=10时需w₀≳16λ或γ=1时需270λ才使异常信号占主导。

Conclusion: 电子穿越激光脉冲后的自旋旋转是一个纯几何效应（holonomy），其大小直接度量光脉冲的螺旋度。该结果是1960年代Ternov-Bagrov-Klimenko工作的精确推广，与中性磁偶极子的取向记忆效应（Oblak-Seraj）具有相同的面积定律，但带电电子的Volkov动力学使g/2耦合完全抵消，仅留下被抑制的异常磁矩通道。

Abstract: We compute the spin rotation that survives after a relativistic electron has crossed a plane-wave laser pulse of finite duration. In the interaction picture built on the exact $g=2$ evolution, the Thomas-Bargmann-Michel-Telegdi equation becomes parallel transport by a connection with constant coefficients on the polarization plane, and the pulse enters only through the closed curve that the transverse vector potential traces there. The net rotation is the holonomy of that connection: an angle $-\frac{1}{2}a_e^2\mathcal{A}$ about the propagation direction, with $a_e=(g-2)/2$ the anomaly and $\mathcal{A}$ twice the signed area enclosed by the curve. That area is the spin angular momentum the pulse carries per unit area, so the rotation measures the helicity of the light. Reduction of the residual dynamics to a rotation coupled through the anomaly alone is an exact result of the 1960s [Ternov, Bagrov, and Klimenko, Sov. Phys. J. 11, 29 (1968); Bagrov and Gitman, The Dirac Equation and its Solutions (De Gruyter, Berlin, 2014), Sec. 5.3], which yields two closed-form cases; the area law is the general second order that those two cases bound. The same area governs the orientation memory of a neutral magnetic dipole [Oblak and Seraj, Phys. Rev. D 109, 044037 (2024)] with a coupling of order unity. For a charged electron on a Volkov orbit the coupling $g/2$ cancels identically, which suppresses the rotation by $1.3\times10^{-6}$ and leaves a channel with no $g$-independent part. We verify the cancellation at $g=2$ over 180 pulse configurations and the area law over 89 more. Finite focusing restores that part at second order in $1/kw_0$, and it exceeds the anomalous signal unless $w_0\gtrsim16λ$ at $γ=10$, or $270λ$ at $γ=1$.

</details>


### [103] [Machine Learning is Good for Physics - and Vice Versa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05812)
*Michael Krämer,Tilman Plehn*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论了科学AI正在快速变革基础物理学研究，并挑战基础物理方法论的界定性方面。作者分析了这一转型的机遇与风险，认为只要保持对各自领域科学方法论的清醒认识，AI与基础物理的紧密互动能带来令人兴奋的益处。文章聚焦统计验证和泛化理论描述两个关键方面，目标是在海量数据中发现新物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学AI正在深刻改变基础物理研究方式，这一转型既有巨大潜力也伴随风险。作者希望系统性地审视这种变革，明确AI与基础物理深度融合的机遇和潜在危险，以确保科学方法论的正确性。

Method: 本文采用理论分析与批判性讨论的方法，从基础物理方法论的角度出发，重点考察两个核心方面：统计验证（处理海量数据中的显著性检验问题）和泛化理论描述（构建能够超越训练数据范围的普适性理论），以此探讨AI在基础物理中的角色与局限。

Result: 研究表明，AI与基础物理的紧密互动能够带来显著益处，但前提是必须保持对两个领域各自科学方法的清醒认知。对统计验证和泛化理论描述的系统性审视有助于在利用AI处理海量数据的同时，避免陷入方法论上的误区。

Conclusion: 作者认为，科学AI对基础物理的转型是双刃剑，既有令人兴奋的机遇也有不可忽视的风险。只有保持对科学方法论的警觉，AI才能在基础物理研究中真正发挥其发现新物理的潜力。

Abstract: Scientific AI is rapidly transforming fundamental physics research and challenging defining aspects of the fundamental physics methodology. We discuss opportunities and dangers of this transformation and find exciting benefits from a close interaction between AI and fundamental physics, provided that we remain aware of the scientific methodologies of the respective fields. For fundamental physics, we discuss two such aspects: statistical validation and a generalizing theory description, both with the goal of discovering new physics in vast datasets.

</details>


### [104] [Descoped and Upscoped FCC-ee Running Scenarios in the SMEFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05874)
*Eugenia Celada,Elie Hammou,Jaco ter Hoeve,Marion O. A. Thomas*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在SMEFT框架下，系统分析了FCC-ee的不同降级和升级运行方案对新物理灵敏度的投影影响，重点探讨了移除顶夸克运行、降低束流功率、减少对撞点等降级情景以及均匀增亮度和定向增强顶夸克运行两种升级情景，结果表明顶夸克运行对FCC-ee的物理潜力至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCC-ee作为未来正负电子对撞机，其运行方案可能因资金、运营成本等因素面临调整。作者希望量化不同降级/升级方案对SMEFT新物理灵敏度的影响，为决策者提供物理学依据，同时阐明顶夸克运行在FCC-ee物理计划中的不可替代地位。

Method: 以基线FCC-ee方案为参照，系统设计多种运行情景：(1)降级场景——依次移除顶夸克run、将束流功率从50MW降至30MW、减少两个对撞点IP，并将顶夸克质量精度的损失通过参数不确定性传播到SMEFT拟合中；(2)升级场景——均匀提升所有能量run的亮度，或仅针对顶夸克run定向增强亮度；(3)分阶段顶夸克run场景——后期资金到位时部分恢复顶夸克运行。所有情景均在SMEFT框架下进行灵敏度投影，并与(HL)-LHC测量进行互补性对比。

Result: 分析表明，移除顶夸克run将使FCC-ee的大部分物理潜力丧失，说明顶夸克运行是FCC-ee新物理灵敏度的核心支柱；降低束流功率和减少IP也会显著削弱灵敏度。两种升级方案中，定向增强顶夸克run的亮度比均匀增亮更有效。FCC-ee与(HL)-LHC之间存在重要的互补性，且这种互补性在不同运行情景下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 作者明确指出，FCC-ee的物理发现潜力在很大程度上依赖于顶夸克运行，这一运行是方案的核心组成部分，不应被轻易削减。研究同时为如何在资金约束下优化FCC-ee运行方案提供了定量的物理依据。

Abstract: We present projections for the sensitivity to new physics of descoped and upscoped FCC-ee scenarios in the framework of the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT). Starting from the baseline FCC-ee programme, we analyse the subsequent impact of, first, removing the top-quark run, then reducing the beam power from 50 MW to 30 MW, and finally removing two interaction points (IPs), in all cases propagating the resulting loss in top-quark mass precision through increased parametric uncertainties. We also analyse the possibility of a staged top-quark run, where the top-quark run is partially restored in case funding becomes available only at a later stage. Motivated by the trade-off between beam power and operational costs, we also consider two upscoped scenarios, either through a uniform increase in luminosity across all energy runs, or through a luminosity enhancement targeting exclusively the top-quark run. We explore the sensitivity and limitations of each scenario and highlight in particular the complementarity between the FCC-ee projections and the (HL)-LHC measurements. By comparing the physics impact of the different running scenarios with the FCC-ee baseline, we show explicitly the importance of the top-quark run, indicating that a substantial fraction of the FCC-ee physics potential depends on it.

</details>


### [105] [QCD analytic coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05894)
*I. A. Zemlyakov,I. L. Chuev,A. V. Kotikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文简要概述了QCD解析耦合方法，主要基于文献[1]的结果，并将其应用于π介子-光子跃迁形状因子的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD解析耦合是处理微扰QCD在低能区失效问题的重要方法，将其应用于π介子-光子跃迁形状因子可以检验该方法在强子物理中的适用性。

Method: 基于QCD解析耦合理论，遵循文献[1]中得到的解析耦合表达式，并将其应用到π介子-光子跃迁形状因子的计算中。

Result: 展示了QCD解析耦合方法在π介子-光子跃迁形状因子计算中的应用结果。

Conclusion: QCD解析耦合方法能够有效地应用于π介子-光子跃迁形状因子等强子物理过程的计算。

Abstract: A brief overview of QCD analytic coupling is presented, mainly following the results obtained in [1]. An application to the pion-photon transition form factor is demonstrated.

</details>


### [106] [Impact of dimension-8 SMEFT operators on baryogenesis via sphaleron decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05950)
*Kiyoto Ogawa,Masanori Tanaka*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在标准模型有效场论框架下研究了一种称为"sphalerogenesis"的重子产生机制是否能解释宇宙观测到的重子不对称性。作者引入了七个破坏CP的维度8算子（由希格斯双态和SU(2)_L规范场构成），发现其中五个可单独解释观测到的重子不对称性，同时满足对撞机或电子电偶极矩测量的实验约束。进一步研究发现，在存在破坏CP的维度6算子时，当维度8算子在一圈级别产生时，其贡献可与维度6算子相当，表明在sphalerogenesis中圈阶计数与质量维度计数同样重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙观测到的重子不对称性（BAU）是粒子物理和宇宙学中的重大未解之谜。标准模型中的电弱重子产生机制（sphaleron过程）本身不足以解释观测到的重子不对称性，因此需要探索超出标准模型的有效场论扩展是否能为这一问题的解决提供可行方案。

Method: 作者在标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）框架下，构造了七个由希格斯双态和SU(2)_L规范场构成的CP破坏维度8算子，系统分析这些算子在电弱sphaleron跃迁CP不对称解耦过程中对重子不对称产生的影响，并考虑对撞机和电子电偶极矩（EDM）等实验约束。同时研究了这些维度8算子与CP破坏维度6算子共存时的相互作用。

Result: 五个维度8算子能够单独解释观测到的重子不对称性且满足现有实验约束。当维度8算子与维度6算子共存时，若维度8算子在一圈级别产生，其贡献可以与维度6算子相当甚至可比，说明循环阶数对贡献大小的影响可能超过算子的质量维度。

Conclusion: Sphalerogenesis作为一种重子产生机制在SMEFT框架内是可行的，且高阶维度算子（维度8）的贡献不可忽视。在评估有效场论算子对重子产生的影响时，不仅要考虑算子的质量维度，还需考虑其产生的循环阶数——循环阶数的计数在sphalerogenesis中可能比质量维度的计数更为关键。

Abstract: We investigate whether a baryogenesis mechanism known as sphalerogenesis can account for the observed baryon asymmetry of the Universe within the Standard Model effective field theory. In this scenario, the baryon asymmetry is generated through the $CP$-asymmetric decoupling of electroweak (EW) sphaleron-like transitions. We introduce seven $CP$-violating dimension-8 operators constructed from the Higgs doublet and the $SU(2)_L$ gauge fields and show that five of them can individually account for the observed baryon asymmetry with satisfying experimental constraints from colliders or electron electric dipole moment measurements. We further study their impact in the presence of a $CP$-violating dimension-6 operator. We find that the dimension-8 contributions can be comparable to the dimension-6 contribution when the dimension-8 operators are generated at one loop, demonstrating that loop-order counting can be as important as canonical mass-dimension counting in sphalerogenesis.

</details>


### [107] [Infrared singularities and the collinear limits of multi-leg scattering amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06157)
*Sebastian Jaskiewicz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了无质量散射振幅在多粒子共线极限下的因子化结构，证明了通过四圈阶，由严格两粒子共线因子化对无质量软反常维度施加的要求足以保证任意多粒子共线极限下的因子化。对于包含大质量色粒子的振幅，通过考虑三个无质量粒子的共线极限，导出了对软反常维度的新约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射振幅在特殊运动学极限（如软极限和共线极限）下具有因子化结构，理解多粒子共线极限中各阶微扰修正的因子化机制对于研究高圈阶振幅的结构至关重要，特别是要探明严格共线因子化如何实现以及需要软反常维度满足何种约束条件。

Method: 通过分析无质量振幅在多粒子共线极限下的因子化性质，证明两粒子共线极限中严格共线因子化对软反常维度施加的结构要求足以保证任意多粒子共线极限下的因子化（验证至四圈阶）。在此基础上，进一步考虑含有一个大质量色粒子的振幅，通过分析三个无质量粒子共线的情形来推导新的约束条件。

Result: 证明了在四圈阶下，两粒子共线极限中严格共线因子化对无质量软反常维度结构的要求可以推广到任何多粒子共线极限，保证其因子化性质成立。同时，对于含大质量粒子的振幅，通过研究三个无质量粒子的共线极限，导出了软反常维度需要满足的新约束条件。

Conclusion: 多粒子共线因子化性质由两粒子共线极限下的约束所保证，这一结论验证至四圈阶。此外，大质量色粒子存在时需要对软反常维度施加额外约束，这为研究包含重粒子的散射振幅的高阶结构提供了新的理论限制。

Abstract: Scattering amplitudes admit a factorised structure in special kinematic limits, such as the soft and collinear limits. In this work, we investigate the multi-particle collinear limits of massless amplitudes at high perturbative orders, focusing on the exploration of the mechanisms via which strict collinear factorisation of $n$-particle scattering amplitudes is realised when $m$ particles become collinear. We show through four loops that the requirements on the structure of the massless soft anomalous dimension that are imposed by strict collinear factorisation in all two-particle collinear limits are enough to guarantee factorisation also in any multi-particle collinear limit. Demanding that strict collinear factorisation of massless partons is satisfied also for amplitudes that contain a massive coloured particle, we derive new constraints on the soft anomalous dimension by considering the collinear limit of three massless particles.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [On the motivic cohomology of some singular rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05220)
*Tess Bouis*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用非A^1不变动机上同调，以及整p进Hodge理论替代标准K-理论中的迹方法，证明了几类奇异环（如Z/p^n、Z[x]/(x^e)及C(X;C)）代数K-理论的动机细化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知某些奇异环的代数K-理论计算已经存在，但缺乏动机层面的细化。本文旨在通过非A^1不变动机上同调给出这些计算的动机细化版本，并引入整p进Hodge理论作为传统迹方法的替代工具。

Method: 采用非A^1不变的动机上同调框架，并运用整p进Hodge理论来替代K-理论中标准的迹方法（如拓扑Hochschild同调及相关技术），从而实现对奇异环K-理论的动机层面细化。

Result: 成功为形如Z/p^n、Z[x]/(x^e)以及C(X;C)（X为紧Hausdorff空间）的奇异环的代数K-理论建立了动机细化结果，且证明了整p进Hodge理论在该情境下作为迹方法替代方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 非A^1不变动机上同调与整p进Hodge理论的结合，为奇异环K-理论提供了一条新的、不依赖传统迹方法的精细化计算途径，拓展了动机上同调在该领域的应用范围。

Abstract: Using non-$\mathbb{A}^1$-invariant motivic cohomology, we prove motivic refinements of certain known computations of the algebraic $K$-theory of singular rings, such as rings of the form $\mathbb{Z}/p^n$, $\mathbb{Z}[x]/(x^e)$, and $\mathscr{C}(X;\mathbb{C})$ for $X$ a compact Hausdorff space. These refinements are made possible by the use of integral $p$-adic Hodge theory, as a replacement for the standard use of trace methods in $K$-theory.

</details>


### [109] [Elliptic matroids and modular curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05299)
*Matthew Baker*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了秩3拟阵T_n（基为Z/nZ上非零和三元子集）的k-实现类与模曲线X_1(n)^∘的k-点之间的自然双射，并将其升级为Z[1/n]上的概形同构。通过形变理论论证验证Artin局部环上的点同构，从而为X_1(n)^∘在Z[1/n]上赋予拟阵实现空间模型。由此，T_p在Q上的不可表示性与Mazur关于椭圆曲线有理挠点的著名定理的素数阶情形等价。附录中将拟阵实现空间从Z上的仿射概形升级为F_1^±上的仿射带概形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作源于Borisov和Roulleau关于复域上X_1(n)^∘与拟阵T_n实现空间之间联系的定理。作者希望将该对应从C推广到任意域，并从集合论双射提升为概形同构，同时希望建立模曲线的新模型以及拟阵实现空间与数论经典定理（Mazur定理）之间的联系。

Method: 作者采用纯代数和关联理论（incidence-theoretic）的方法证明域值对应，核心工具是形变理论（deformation-theoretic argument），通过在Artin局部环上验证点的同构，从而将双射升级为Z[1/n]上的概形同构。附录中使用Baker-Jin-Lorscheid的带概形（band scheme）理论将实现空间提升为F_1^±上的结构。

Result: 对n≥10，建立了域k上X_1(n)^∘(k)与T_n的k-实现重定标类之间的自然双射（要求char(k)∤n），并升级为Z[1/n]上的概形同构。由此X_1(n)^∘在Z[1/n]上获得自然的拟阵实现空间模型；对素数p≥11，T_p在Q上的不可表示性与Mazur定理的素数阶情形等价。

Conclusion: 该论文将模曲线与拟阵实现空间之间的联系从复域推广到一般域及整系数层，建立了概形层面的同构，并通过形变理论解决了Artin局部环上点的验证问题。这为模曲线提供了新的算术模型，同时揭示了拟阵实现与经典数论定理之间的深层联系。附录的带概形升级为F_1^±上的几何提供了新视角。

Abstract: For $n\geq 4$, let $T_n$ be the rank-3 matroid on $\mathbb{Z}/n\mathbb{Z}$ whose bases are the three-element non-zero-sum subsets. Let $X_1(n)^\circ$ denote the open subscheme of the modular curve $X_1(n)$ obtained by removing the cusps corresponding to reducible Néron polygons. For $n \geq 10$, we give a purely algebraic and incidence-theoretic proof that, for every field $k$ with $\mathrm{char}(k)$ not dividing $n$, there is a natural bijection between $X_1(n)^\circ(k)$ and rescaling classes of $k$-realizations of $T_n$. For $k = \mathbb{C}$, this recovers a theorem of Borisov and Roulleau.
  We then upgrade the field-valued correspondence to an isomorphism of schemes over $\mathbb{Z}[1/n]$. The main new ingredient is a deformation-theoretic argument which allows us to verify the isomorphism on points valued in Artinian local rings. As consequences, the modular curve $X_1(n)^\circ$ acquires a natural model over $\mathbb{Z}[1/n]$ as a matroid realization space, and, for primes $p \geq 11$, the non-representability of $T_p$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ is equivalent to the prime-order case of Mazur's celebrated theorem on rational torsion points of elliptic curves.
  In an appendix, we explain how to upgrade the realization space of a matroid from an affine scheme over $\mathbb{Z}$ to an affine band scheme (in the sense of Baker-Jin-Lorscheid) over $\mathbb{F}_1^{\pm}$.

</details>


### [110] [The weak Markus--Yamabe conjecture fails in dimension 14](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05392)
*Álvaro Castañeda,Gerardo Honorato,Francisco Valenzuela-Henríquez*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了弱Markus-Yamabe猜想在维度n≥14时均失败，通过链实现定理将Keller映射转化为谱为{-1}的向量场，并结合雅可比猜想的最新反例构造出了具体的Hurwitz向量场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱Markus-Yamabe猜想是动力系统与雅可比猜想之间的重要联系，判定其是否成立对于理解多项式向量场的全局渐近行为至关重要。

Method: 首先证明了一个链实现定理：任意分量次数为d_i的多项式Keller映射F=I+H在R^n上可以构造出R^N上的显式多项式向量场，其中N=Σmax(d_i,2)-n，该向量场的雅可比矩阵处处谱为{-1}，其奇点与F的任意给定纤维双射对应。然后将该定理应用于雅可比猜想的最新反例，得到R^14上的七次Hurwitz向量场；再通过三次稳定的秩约减去齐次化，独立构造出R^18上的三次Hurwitz场。

Result: 在维度n≥14上构造出了弱Markus-Yamabe猜想的反例，具体包括R^14上一个七次Hurwitz向量场（含三个有理奇点）和R^18上一个三次Hurwitz场。维度3≤n≤13的情形仍然开放。

Conclusion: 弱Markus-Yamabe猜想在维度n≥14时是错误的，这通过Keller映射与Hurwitz向量场之间的链实现联系得以证明，将问题维度从雅可比猜想维度转移到向量场维度。

Abstract: We show that the weak Markus--Yamabe conjecture fails in every dimension $n\geq14$. We first prove a chain realization theorem: every polynomial Keller map $F=\I+H$ of $\R^n$ with component degrees $d_1,\ldots,d_n$ yields an explicit polynomial vector field on $\R^N$, with $N=\sum_i\max(d_i,2)-n$, whose Jacobian matrix has spectrum $\{-1\}$ at every point and whose singularities are in bijection with any prescribed fiber of $F$. Applied to the recent counterexample to the Jacobian conjecture, this gives a Hurwitz vector field of degree seven on $\R^{14}$ with three rational singularities. A rank-reduced dehomogenization of the associated cubic stabilization gives, independently, a Hurwitz field of degree three on $\R^{18}$. The dimensions $3\leq n\leq13$ remain open.

</details>


### [111] [Elliptic curve counting in toric threefolds: virtual, enumerative, and tropical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05486)
*Sae Koyama*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究环面三维流形中椭圆曲线的枚举几何，引入称为"well-spaced counts"的整数不变量，可通过R^3中的well-spaced属一热带曲线进行研究。通过将其与对数退化公式比较，建立了对数虚拟不变量与几何不变量之间的显式关系，得到了Getzler–Pandharipande关于P^3中椭圆曲线公式的对数类比。作为应用，证明了对足够大度数，P^3关于其环面边界（相对于P^3）的虚拟对数不变量严格小于普通Gromov–Witten不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究环面三维流形中椭圆曲线的枚举几何，希望建立对数Gromov–Witten理论与几何计数之间的显式联系，并通过对数类比推广Getzler–Pandharipande的经典结果，从而深入理解对数虚拟不变量与普通Gromov–Witten不变量之间的差异。

Method: 利用well-spaced属一热带曲线研究整数不变量，将其与对数退化公式进行比较，建立对数虚拟不变量与这些几何不变量之间的显式关系，从而得到对数版本的Getzler–Pandharipande公式。

Result: 得到了对数版本的Getzler–Pandharipande公式，并证明了对于充分大的度数，P^3相对于其环面边界的虚拟对数不变量严格小于普通Gromov–Witten不变量。文中包含多个计算示例。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了几何计数与对数虚拟不变量之间的清晰桥梁，揭示了对数不变量与普通Gromov–Witten不变量在大度数情形下的严格差异，为理解两类不变量之间的关系提供了新的视角和具体公式。

Abstract: We study the enumerative geometry of elliptic curves in toric threefolds. We consider enumerative integer invariants, called well-spaced counts, which can be studied using well-spaced genus-one tropical curves in $\mathbb{R}^3$. By comparing this with the logarithmic degeneration formula, we obtain an explicit relationship between logarithmic virtual invariants and these geometric invariants. The result is a logarithmic analogue of a formula of Getzler--Pandharipande for elliptic curves in $\mathbb{P}^3$. As an application, we show that the virtual logarithmic invariants for $\mathbb{P}^3$ with respect to its toric boundary are strictly less than the ordinary Gromov--Witten invariants once the degree is sufficiently large. Several examples are included.

</details>


### [112] [On the Numerical Terao Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05662)
*Piotr Pokora*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了偶数次圆锥-直线排列在仅含ADE奇点情况下满足数值Terao猜想，但通过构造两个度数为九的反例(各含七条直线和一个光滑圆锥，具有相同的弱组合信息)证明了在拟齐次设置下该猜想不成立——其中一个排列是自由的(指数(4,4))，另一个是近似自由的(指数(3,6))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值Terao猜想是超平面排列理论中的重要猜想，它断言排列的自由性(由Tutte多项式刻画)与其组合信息(相交格)相关。此前该猜想在一般情形下是否成立是开放问题，本文旨在探究该猜想在圆锥-直线排列这一特殊类别中的有效边界。

Method: 首先对具有偶数度的圆锥-直线排列进行分类，利用ADE奇点的性质证明数值Terao猜想成立。然后在更广泛的拟齐次设置中，通过构造具体的度数为九的排列实例(七条直线加一个圆锥)，利用其弱组合信息W(CL)相同但代数结构不同(自由vs近似自由)来构造反例。

Result: 定理1：偶数度圆锥-直线排列(仅含ADE奇点)满足数值Terao猜想。定理2：在拟齐次条件下，若允许普通四重点(ordinary quadruple points)，数值Terao猜想不成立——构造了度为9的一对反例，具有相同弱组合信息(7,1; 8A1+D4+4X9)，但一个为自由排列(指数(4,4))，另一个为近似自由排列(指数(3,6))。

Conclusion: 数值Terao猜想的有效边界被精确刻画：在ADE奇点的偶数次圆锥-直线排列中成立，但一旦引入普通四重点并考虑拟齐次情形，猜想即被推翻。该反例也反驳了数值Terao猜想最强形式，揭示了自由性由更精细的代数结构而非仅组合信息决定。

Abstract: We prove that the Numerical Terao Conjecture holds for even-degree conic-line arrangements having only ADE singularities. We then show that the conjecture fails in the broader quasi-homogeneous setting once ordinary quadruple points are admitted, by constructing a degree-nine counterexample, each consisting of seven lines and one smooth conic, with the same weak combinatorics \[ W(\mathcal{CL}) = (7,1;\,8A_1+D_4+4X_9). \] We show that one curve is free with exponents $(4,4)$, whereas the other is nearly free with exponents $(3,6)$. This yields a counterexample to the strongest known formulation of the Numerical Terao Conjecture.

</details>


### [113] [Real Structures in the Moduli of Projective Models of K3-Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05941)
*Çisem Güneş Aktaş*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有给定简单奇点的K3曲面族是否包含实代数曲面，开发了一种适用于所有极化的显式且统一的算法来检测任何等奇点层中的实代表。作为主要应用，解决了空间四次曲面的问题，证明除三种例外类型外，所有实等奇点层都包含实曲面；该方法还重新审视了平面六次曲线的情形，恢复了已知的唯一无实代表的简单六次曲线实层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的核心动机是解决一个基本的实代数几何问题：给定具有预设简单奇点的K3曲面复族，其中是否包含实代数曲面。此前该问题缺乏统一的处理框架，尤其是在所有极化条件下以及任意等奇点层中的实代表存在性判定。

Method: 本文提出了一个显式且统一的算法，用于在任意等奇点层中检测实代表，该算法对所有的极化均适用。该方法建立在K3曲面族的等奇点层结构及其实形式理论之上。

Result: 作为主要应用，作者完整解决了空间四次曲面的实可表示性问题，证明了除了三种例外类型外，所有实等奇点层都包含实曲面。此外，该方法还统一地恢复了平面六次曲线中唯一已知的无实代表的简单实层。

Conclusion: 本文为K3曲面族等奇点层中的实代表检测提供了一个通用的算法框架，不仅在空间四次曲面情形下完整解决了该问题（仅剩三种例外），还以统一视角重新确认了平面六次曲线情形中已知的异常层，表明该算法具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: This paper investigates when a complex family of K3-surfaces with prescribed simple singularities contains real algebraic surfaces. We develop an explicit and uniform algorithm for detecting real representatives in any equisingular stratum, valid for all polarizations. As a primary application, we resolve the problem for spatial quartics, showing that all real equisingular strata contain real surfaces except for three exceptional types, thereby completing the study of real representability in this case. Our method also revisits the analogous phenomenon in the realm of plane sextics, recovering the unique known real stratum of simple sextics without real representatives.

</details>


### [114] [About finite differential tropical basis for linear ODE's](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06018)
*Stefano Mereta,Alejandro Vargas*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出了关于线性常微分方程热带化的若干开放问题，主要目标是开发计算经典解收敛半径的方法，并通过研究有限微分热带基的存在性来探索二、三阶方程的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的核心动机是开发计算线性ODE经典解收敛半径的新方法，为此需要刻画哪些方程类允许有限微分热带基的存在，这是实现热带化方法应用的关键前提。

Method: 本文采用理论探索的方式，首先提出关于线性ODE热带化的多个开放问题，然后通过考察二阶和三阶方程的情况进行初步探索，以检验和推进有限微分热带基这一概念（由Fink和Toghani于2022年引入）的应用。

Result: 研究初步探索了二阶和三阶线性ODE的情况，为后续完全刻画允许有限微分热带基的方程类别奠定了基础，但目前仍处于开放问题的提出和初步考察阶段。

Conclusion: 本文识别出热带化线性ODE方法中关键的理论空白，特别是关于有限微分热带基的方程类刻画问题，并通过对低阶情形的研究为该方向提供了初步见解和未来研究方向。

Abstract: We formulate several open questions regarding the tropicalization of linear ODEs, aiming primarily to develop methods for calculating the radius of convergence of their classical solutions. To this aim it is of foremost importance to characterize the classes of equations that admit a finite differential tropical basis, as introduced in (Fink and Toghani, 2022). Our initial exploration examines the second- and third-order cases.

</details>


### [115] [Fundamental groups of the complements to reducible curves on smooth surfaces with restricted classes of components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06136)
*A. Libgober*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对光滑单连通射影曲面上曲线束的等奇异族进行分类，这些曲线束的对偶簇次数至多为6，且包含足够多的成员，其不可约分支为超平面截面或其不可约分支。由此描述了此类曲面上补空间的基本群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望理解光滑单连通射影曲面上曲线束的等奇异族分类，并进一步刻画这些曲面上曲线补空间的基本群结构，特别是当基本群存在到秩大于6的自由群的本质满射时的情形。

Method: 将对偶簇次数至多为6且成员包含足够多超平面截面分支的曲线束等奇异族进行分类，利用这一分类结果推导补空间基本群的性质。

Result: 完成了满足条件的曲线束等奇异族的完整分类，并在此基础上描述了相应曲面上的曲线补空间的基本群，特别是存在到秩大于6自由群的本质满射的情形。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了曲线束等奇异族分类与补空间基本群描述之间的联系，为理解光滑单连通射影曲面上曲线补空间的基本群结构提供了新的分类框架。

Abstract: We classify equisingular families of pencils of curves on smooth simply connected projective surfaces whose dual varieties have degree at most 6, and which contain sufficiently large number of members which irreducible components are either hyperplane sections or irreducible components of hyperplane sections. As a consequence, we describe the fundamental groups of complements to curves on such surfaces whose irreducible components satisfy the above condition and whose fundamental groups admit an essential surjection onto a free group of rank greater than 6.

</details>


### [116] [Compactification Independence of the Irregular Hodge Filtration on Deligne-Mumford Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06234)
*Haoxu Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对光滑分离、有限型的Deligne-Mumford栈$(mathscr U,w)$（带正则函数$w$）上定义的Yu滤过的紧化不变性。作者利用好分解和栈性弱因子化，将比较约化为沿边界除子的爆破与根操作，证明了Yu复形与Kontsevich格子在爆破和根操作下的滤过比较定理，从而得到滤过不依赖于紧化的选择。该结果逐扇区应用于Harder和Lee的栈性Clarke镜像对研究，表明orbifold滤过及不规则Hodge数仅依赖于栈性Landau-Ginzburg模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yu通过紧化定义了twisted de Rham上同调上的滤过，且同一紧化给出计算该滤过的Kontsevich格。然而紧化上的$w$延拓可能只是有理函数，且仅在极点除子附近施加局部非退化条件。关键问题在于该滤过是否不依赖于紧化的选择，这对于定义栈性Landau-Ginzburg模型的不变量（如orbifold不规则Hodge数）至关重要。

Method: 证明分为两步：(1) 使用好分解（good resolutions）和栈性弱因子化（stacky weak factorization），将任意两个紧化之间的比较约化为两类基本操作：沿边界除子的爆破（blowups）和根操作（roots）；(2) 分别对Yu复形和Kontsevich复形建立这两类操作下的滤过比较定理，从而逐步骤保持滤过不変。

Result: 证明了Yu滤过不依赖于紧化的选择。对于爆破和根操作，分别建立了Yu复形与Kontsevich复形的滤过比较定理。该紧化独立性可逐扇区应用于Harder-Lee的栈性Clarke镜像对设置，从而所得orbifold滤过与不规则Hodge数仅依赖于栈性Landau-Ginzburg模型本身。

Conclusion: 本文通过栈性弱因子化与基本操作的比较定理，确立了Yu滤过在栈性Landau-Ginzburg模型上的典范性（紧化无关性），进而验证了Harder-Lee所定义的orbifold不规则Hodge数的良定义性，为栈性镜像对称研究提供了严格的基础。

Abstract: Let $(\mathscr U,w)$ be a smooth separated Deligne--Mumford stack of finite type over $\mathbb C$, with a regular function $w$. Following Yu, one can use a compactification to define a filtration on its twisted de Rham cohomology. The same compactification gives Kontsevich lattices that compute this filtration. The extension of $w$ on the compactification may be only rational. Only a local nondegeneracy condition near the polar divisor is imposed.
  We prove that the resulting filtration does not depend on the compactification. The proof uses good resolutions and stacky weak factorization. This reduces the comparison to blowups and roots along boundary divisors. Filtered comparison theorems for the Yu and Kontsevich complexes are established for both operations.
  Harder and Lee use orbifold irregular Hodge numbers in their study of stacky Clarke mirror pairs. Compactification independence applies sector by sector to their setting. Thus the resulting orbifold filtration and irregular Hodge numbers depend only on the stack Landau--Ginzburg model.

</details>


### [117] [Frobenius-orbit slicing and uniform elimination of positive-dimensional singular loci](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06273)
*Yutong Zhang,Yaoran Yang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究算术Bertini定理中主部分射流零方案的概率界。对固定整射影子概形X（在Z上光滑，相对维数r），证明m阶主部分射流的限制具有正维数零方案的概率不超过C(d+1)^{N_m}p^{-λ_m(d)}，其中N_m和λ_m(d)为显式常数。该结果解决了Poonen的算术Bertini猜想5.2，并给出了多独立超曲面的Jacobian秩退化概率界的双重估计。证明依赖Q-进滤波分解、三角正规Taylor块和Jacobian枢轴图等技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在算术几何中，Bertini定理的定量版本（即估计一般超平面/超曲面截面奇异位置的概率）具有基础性意义。Poonen提出了算术Bertini猜想，但此前缺乏对m阶主部分射流（higher-order principal-parts jets）情形的精确概率界。本文旨在填补这一空白，为高阶射流情形提供显式概率上界，并解决该猜想。

Method: 本文采用的技术工具包括：1）滤波Q-进分解（filtered Q-adic decompositions），用于将度d形式在p进意义下分层处理；2）三角正规Taylor块（triangular normal Taylor blocks），用于控制射流在奇异点的行为；3）Jacobian枢轴图（Jacobian-pivot charts），以统一定义和控制复杂度；4）对p^{-λ_m(d)}形式的指数界进行精细的计数与概率估计。

Result: 获得的主要结果为：1）对每个固定m≥1，m阶主部分射流正维数零方案的概率界为C(d+1)^{N_m}p^{-λ_m(d)}；2）m=1时退化为Bertini奇异轨迹估计C(d+1)^{r+1}p^{-⌈d/2⌉}；3）解决了Poonen的算术Bertini猜想5.2；4）提升度阈值后对任意固定A>0可达到p^{-A}的界；5）对c≤r个独立超曲面，给出Jacobian秩退化轨迹概率的双重上界（逐项和与最小值界）。

Conclusion: 本文在定量算术Bertini定理方面取得了突破性进展，不仅解决了Poonen的猜想，而且将结果推广到高阶射流和多超曲面的情形。得到的概率界具有显式常数，可用于后续的算术统计与计数应用。证明中引入的滤波Q-进分解和Jacobian枢轴图等技术为相关问题提供了新的方法论工具。

Abstract: Let $\cX\subseteq\Pj^n_{\Z}$ be a fixed integral quasiprojective subscheme, smooth over $\Z$ of relative dimension $r$. For each fixed $m\ge1$, we bound the probability that the $m$th principal-parts jet of the restriction of a uniform degree-$d$ form to $\cX_p$ has a positive-dimensional zero scheme. The bound is $C(d+1)^{N_m}p^{-λ_m(d)}$, where $N_m=\binom{r+m}{m}$ and $λ_m(d)=\floor{m(d+1)/(m+1)}$. For $m=1$, this gives the Bertini singular-locus estimate $C(d+1)^{r+1}p^{-\ceil{d/2}}$. It settles Poonen's arithmetic Bertini Conjecture~5.2 and, after increasing the degree threshold, yields $p^{-A}$ for every fixed $A>0$. For $c\le r$ independent hypersurfaces, the probability of a positive-dimensional Jacobian rank-degeneracy locus is bounded both by $C\sum_i(d_i+1)^{r+1}p^{-\ceil{d_i/2}}$ and by $C'(d_{\min}+1)^{r+1}p^{-\ceil{d_{\min}/2}}$. The proof uses filtered $Q$-adic decompositions, triangular normal Taylor blocks, and Jacobian-pivot charts of uniformly controlled complexity.

</details>


### [118] [The graded Betti numbers are not tropical invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06284)
*Noah Laikin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了分次Betti数不是齐次理想热带化的不变量，也不是赋值域上射影代数簇热带化的不变量。作者通过构造一族饱和理想来实现这一结论：这族理想都实现同一个热带理想，但其中有一个成员的Betti表与其他成员不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究热带化在代数几何中的信息保留能力，特别是探究热带化是否保留了同调不变量（如Betti数）。如果Betti数是热带化的不变量，那么可以通过热带化来简化计算；但本文表明事实并非如此。

Method: 构造一族饱和理想，使其对应的热带理想相同，但其中一个成员的Betti表区别于其余成员，从而展示Betti数不是热带化的不变量。

Result: 证明了分次Betti数既不是齐次理想热带化的不变量，也不是射影代数簇热带化的不变量。

Conclusion: 热带化虽然能捕捉某些几何信息，但无法保留更精细的同调不变性（如分次Betti数），这意味着在热带几何框架下无法仅通过热带化恢复完整的同调结构。

Abstract: We show that the graded Betti numbers are not invariants of the tropicalisation of a homogeneous ideal, or the tropicalisation of a projective algebraic scheme over a valued field. We do this by producing a family of saturated ideals that all realise the same tropical ideal with one member having a distinct Betti table from the rest.

</details>


### [119] [Stability of kernel bundles on projective bundles over curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06365)
*Abel Castorena,George H. Hitching*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明：在模空间中一般的射影丛X（基为亏格g≥3的光滑曲线C）上，对于充分丰富的相对超平面丛O_X(1)，其生成子空间V所对应的核丛是O_X(1)-稳定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究射影丛上线性系统的核丛稳定性，这是代数几何中经典稳定性理论在更一般几何对象上的推广，旨在理解高维簇上向量丛的模空间结构。

Method: 利用模空间的一般性条件，将问题化归为对曲线C、射影丛X和生成子空间V的一般性假设，结合充分丰富条件，通过构造和变形论证核丛的稳定性。

Result: 在C、X和V均为一般模参数且O_X(1)充分丰富时，核丛是O_X(1)-稳定的。

Conclusion: 该结果将经典的曲线情形推广到射影丛的高维设定，给出了核丛稳定性成立的一般充分条件。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a projective bundle over a smooth curve $C$ of genus $g \ge 3$, and consider the relative hyperplane bundle ${\mathcal O}_X (1) \to X$. Let $V \subseteq H^0 ( X , {\mathcal O}_X (1) )$ be a generating subspace. We prove that when $C$, $X$ and $V$ are general in moduli and ${\mathcal O}_X (1)$ is sufficiently ample, the kernel bundle of the system $({\mathcal O}_X (1) , V)$ is ${\mathcal O}_X (1)$-stable.

</details>
